> AGENT

Yes.

`desloppify` already has a detector for this: `flat_dirs`.

- It flags directory overload when any of these are true:
  - `file_count >= 20`
  - `child_dir_count >= 10`
  - `combined_score >= 30` (`files + 3 * child_dirs`)
- Implementation: [flat_dirs.py](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/detectors/flat_dirs.py:7)
- Wired into scan here: [shared_phases.py](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/languages/_framework/base/shared_phases.py:512)

On stalling/failing, I found two separate issues:

1. `review --prepare` stall is real and reproducible  
- `desloppify review --prepare --path .` hangs >90s in this repo.
- Profiling shows it gets stuck in `_abstractions_context` at this regex call: [budget.py](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/intelligence/review/context_holistic/budget.py:95), called from [orchestrator.py](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/intelligence/review/context_holistic/orchestrator.py:83).
- Worst offenders in your tree:
  - `Wan2GP/preprocessing/face_preprocessor.py` (>30s in one regex call)
  - `Wan2GP/shared/attention.py` (~8.9s)

2. `review --run-batches` failures are mostly connectivity  
- Logs show repeated backend disconnects to Codex endpoint:
  - [batch-1.log](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/Headless-Wan2GP/.desloppify/subagents/runs/20260226_042011/logs/batch-1.log:213)

Immediate workaround:
- `desloppify review --prepare --path source` (this completes quickly)
- For batch runs, use external flow: `desloppify review --external-start --external-runner claude`

If you want, I can patch `budget.py` to remove the catastrophic regex path and make this stable on full-repo runs.

{
  "patch": "*** Begin Patch\n*** Update File: /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/intelligence/review/context_holistic/budget.py\n@@\n \"\"\"Sizing and truncation helpers for holistic context payloads.\"\"\"\n \n from __future__ import annotations\n \n+import ast\n import re\n from collections import defaultdict\n from pathlib import Path\n \n from desloppify.file_discovery import rel\n@@\n _DEF_SIGNATURE_RE = re.compile(\n     r\"(?:^|\\n)\\s*(?:async\\s+def|def|async\\s+function|function)\\s+\\w+\\s*\\(([^)]*)\\)\",\n     re.MULTILINE,\n )\n-_PY_PASSTHROUGH_RE = re.compile(\n-    r\"\\bdef\\s+(\\w+)\\s*\\([^)]*\\)\\s*:\\s*(?:\\n\\s*(?:#.*)?\\s*)*\\n?\\s*return\\s+(\\w+)\\s*\\(\",\n-    re.MULTILINE,\n-)\n _TS_PASSTHROUGH_RE = re.compile(\n     r\"\\bfunction\\s+(\\w+)\\s*\\([^)]*\\)\\s*\\{\\s*return\\s+(\\w+)\\s*\\(\",\n     re.MULTILINE,\n )\n@@\n def _score_clamped(raw: float) -> int:\n     \"\"\"Clamp score-like values to [0, 100].\"\"\"\n     return int(max(0, min(100, round(raw))))\n \n \n+def _python_passthrough_target(stmt: ast.stmt) -> str | None:\n+    \"\"\"Return passthrough call target when stmt is `return target(...)`.\"\"\"\n+    if not isinstance(stmt, ast.Return):\n+        return None\n+    value = stmt.value\n+    if not isinstance(value, ast.Call):\n+        return None\n+    target = value.func\n+    if isinstance(target, ast.Name):\n+        return target.id\n+    return None\n+\n+\n+def _find_python_passthrough_wrappers(content: str) -> list[tuple[str, str]]:\n+    \"\"\"Find Python wrapper pairs using AST instead of regex.\n+\n+    Regex-based wrapper detection can hit catastrophic backtracking on large\n+    comment blocks. AST traversal is linear and robust.\n+    \"\"\"\n+    try:\n+        tree = ast.parse(content)\n+    except SyntaxError:\n+        return []\n+\n+    wrappers: list[tuple[str, str]] = []\n+    for node in ast.walk(tree):\n+        if not isinstance(node, ast.FunctionDef | ast.AsyncFunctionDef):\n+            continue\n+\n+        body = list(node.body)\n+        if (\n+            body\n+            and isinstance(body[0], ast.Expr)\n+            and isinstance(body[0].value, ast.Constant)\n+            and isinstance(body[0].value.value, str)\n+        ):\n+            body = body[1:]\n+        if len(body) != 1:\n+            continue\n+\n+        target_name = _python_passthrough_target(body[0])\n+        if target_name and node.name != target_name:\n+            wrappers.append((node.name, target_name))\n+    return wrappers\n+\n+\n def _abstractions_context(file_contents: dict[str, str]) -> dict:\n@@\n-        py_wrappers = [\n-            (wrapper, target)\n-            for wrapper, target in _PY_PASSTHROUGH_RE.findall(content)\n-            if wrapper != target\n-        ]\n+        py_wrappers = _find_python_passthrough_wrappers(content)\n         ts_wrappers = [\n             (wrapper, target)\n             for wrapper, target in _TS_PASSTHROUGH_RE.findall(content)\n             if wrapper != target\n         ]\n*** End Patch\n"
}

> AGENT

You’re right to question it.

`_abstractions_context` is used during `review --prepare` before any subagent runs, not by the subagent itself:

- Prepare flow calls holistic context building: [prepare.py](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/review/prepare.py:75) → [prepare.py](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/intelligence/review/prepare.py:238) → [orchestrator.py](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/intelligence/review/context_holistic/orchestrator.py:83)
- That precomputed context is used to build investigation batches and scoring guidance.

Subagents are intentionally constrained, not free-roaming:

- Prompt says: “Evaluate ONLY listed files and ONLY listed dimensions”: [batch_prompt_template.py](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/review/batch_prompt_template.py:117)
- Workflow also says “read the listed files”: [helpers.py](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/intelligence/review/_prepare/helpers.py:11)

Why it’s designed this way:
- deterministic/reproducible scores
- safe parallelization
- bounded runtime/token cost
- reduced duplicate findings across batches

If you want, I can change this to an “explore related files” mode (still bounded) instead of strict file-only batch scope.

> AGENT

There are two different “access” layers:

- Technical access: subagent processes are launched at repo root with workspace write access (`codex exec ... -C <repo> -s workspace-write`) in [runner_helpers.py](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/review/runner_helpers.py:142).
- Intended review scope: each batch prompt includes a `Files assigned` list and explicitly says “Evaluate ONLY listed files and ONLY listed dimensions” in [batch_prompt_template.py](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/review/batch_prompt_template.py:102) and [batch_prompt_template.py](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/review/batch_prompt_template.py:117).

For your current `Headless-Wan2GP` run, `query.json` has 9 batches with these file counts:
- 22, 80, 75, 2, 21, 16, 7, 80, 80
- 288 unique files total across all batches

Those files are chosen by heuristic batch builders in [prepare_batches.py](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/intelligence/review/prepare_batches.py:400):
- architecture/coupling hotspots
- conventions/error-strategy outliers
- abstraction/dependency hotspots
- auth/ai-debt/migration signals
- package organization
- governance docs + anchors
- mechanical concerns batch
- plus a full-codebase sweep (enabled by default) in [prepare.py](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/intelligence/review/prepare.py:286)

Why: bounded runtime/cost, deterministic coverage, and parallelizable batches.  
The per-batch cap is `review_batch_max_files` (default 80) in [config.py](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/core/config.py:36).

> AGENT

I’ll run a proper sense-check as a code review: verify behavioral correctness, scan for regressions, and run a slightly broader test slice around batch execution/import paths.

> AGENT

**Findings**
1. Medium: Reviewed-file coverage is now potentially inaccurate for non-seed files. The prompt now allows exploring beyond batch seeds ([batch_prompt_template.py](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/review/batch_prompt_template.py:117)), but reviewed files are still derived only from `files_to_read` seeds ([batches.py](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/review/batches.py:253), [batches.py](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/review/batches.py:712)). This can undercount `reviewed_files_count` and miss cache refresh/auto-resolution for actually reviewed non-seed files ([holistic.py](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/intelligence/review/importing/holistic.py:42), [holistic.py](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/intelligence/review/importing/holistic.py:514)).
2. Low: Runtime variance risk is higher because exploration is now open-ended. The new guidance is good, but it can increase long-running batches in some repos unless reviewers self-limit ([batch_prompt_template.py](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/review/batch_prompt_template.py:117)).

**Checks run**
1. `pytest -q desloppify/tests/commands/test_review_batch_core_direct.py desloppify/tests/review/test_prepare_internal_helpers.py` → 12 passed.
2. `pytest -q desloppify/tests/review/test_review_misc.py desloppify/tests/review/test_review_commands.py desloppify/tests/review/test_review_coverage.py desloppify/tests/review/test_review_submodules.py` → 199 passed.
3. `pytest -q desloppify/tests/review/test_review_import_scoring.py desloppify/tests/review/test_review_integrity_direct.py` → 28 passed.
4. `desloppify review --prepare --path .` on `Headless-Wan2GP` confirms new workflow text is emitted.

Implementation is directionally correct and does what you asked (seed files + free exploration). The main follow-up I’d make is adding explicit tracking of explored files so coverage/cache stats stay truthful.

configuration.\"\"\"\n+\n+    label: str\n+    cmd: str\n+    fmt: str\n+    id: str\n+    tier: int\n+    fix_cmd: str\n+\n+\n+def normalize_tool_specs(\n+    raw_tools: list[dict[str, Any]],\n+    *,\n+    supported_formats: set[str],\n+) -> list[ToolSpec]:\n+    \"\"\"Validate and normalize generic tool specs before plugin assembly.\"\"\"\n+    normalized: list[ToolSpec] = []\n+    for idx, raw in enumerate(raw_tools):\n+        if not isinstance(raw, dict):\n+            raise ValueError(f\"tools[{idx}] must be an object\")\n+\n+        label = str(raw.get(\"label\", \"\")).strip()\n+        cmd = str(raw.get(\"cmd\", \"\")).strip()\n+        fmt = str(raw.get(\"fmt\", \"\")).strip()\n+        detector_id = str(raw.get(\"id\", \"\")).strip()\n+        tier_raw = raw.get(\"tier\")\n+\n+        if not label:\n+            raise ValueError(f\"tools[{idx}].label must be a non-empty string\")\n+        if not cmd:\n+            raise ValueError(f\"tools[{idx}].cmd must be a non-empty string\")\n+        if not fmt:\n+            raise ValueError(f\"tools[{idx}].fmt must be a non-empty string\")\n+        if fmt not in supported_formats:\n+            allowed = \", \".join(sorted(supported_formats))\n+            raise ValueError(\n+                f\"tools[{idx}].fmt '{fmt}' is unsupported (expected one of: {allowed})\"\n+            )\n+        if not detector_id:\n+            raise ValueError(f\"tools[{idx}].id must be a non-empty string\")\n+        if isinstance(tier_raw, bool) or not isinstance(tier_raw, int):\n+            raise ValueError(f\"tools[{idx}].tier must be an integer\")\n+        if tier_raw < 1 or tier_raw > 4:\n+            raise ValueError(f\"tools[{idx}].tier must be in range 1..4\")\n+\n+        fix_cmd_raw = raw.get(\"fix_cmd\")\n+        fix_cmd = \"\"\n+        if fix_cmd_raw is not None:\n+            fix_cmd = str(fix_cmd_raw).strip()\n+            if not fix_cmd:\n+                raise ValueError(\n+                    f\"tools[{idx}].fix_cmd must be a non-empty string when provided\"\n+                )\n+\n+        normalized.append(\n+            ToolSpec(\n+                label=label,\n+                cmd=cmd,\n+                fmt=fmt,\n+                id=detector_id,\n+                tier=tier_raw,\n+                fix_cmd=fix_cmd,\n+            )\n+        )\n+    return normalized\n+\n+\n+__all__ = [\"ToolSpec\", \"normalize_tool_specs\"]\n*** End Patch\n"

  "patch": "*** Begin Patch\n*** Add File: /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/core/output_contract.py\n+\"\"\"Shared result contract for non-critical command side effects.\"\"\"\n+\n+from __future__ import annotations\n+\n+from dataclasses import dataclass\n+\n+\n+@dataclass(frozen=True)\n+class OutputResult:\n+    \"\"\"Outcome for best-effort write/generation side-effects.\"\"\"\n+\n+    ok: bool\n+    status: str\n+    message: str | None = None\n+    error_kind: str | None = None\n+\n+\n+__all__ = [\"OutputResult\"]\n*** End Patch\n"
}

-->\"\n+SKILL_END = \"<!-- desloppify-end -->\"\n+\n+# Locations where the skill doc might be installed, relative to project root.\n+SKILL_SEARCH_PATHS = (\n+    \".claude/skills/desloppify/SKILL.md\",\n+    \"AGENTS.md\",\n+    \"CLAUDE.md\",\n+    \".cursor/rules/desloppify.md\",\n+    \".github/copilot-instructions.md\",\n+)\n+\n+# Interface name → (target file, overlay filename, dedicated).\n+# Dedicated files are overwritten entirely; shared files get section replacement.\n+SKILL_TARGETS: dict[str, tuple[str, str, bool]] = {\n+    \"claude\": (\".claude/skills/desloppify/SKILL.md\", \"CLAUDE\", True),\n+    \"codex\": (\"AGENTS.md\", \"CODEX\", False),\n+    \"cursor\": (\".cursor/rules/desloppify.md\", \"CURSOR\", True),\n+    \"copilot\": (\".github/copilot-instructions.md\", \"COPILOT\", False),\n+    \"windsurf\": (\"AGENTS.md\", \"WINDSURF\", False),\n+    \"gemini\": (\"AGENTS.md\", \"GEMINI\", False),\n+}\n+\n+\n+@dataclass\n+class SkillInstall:\n+    \"\"\"Detected skill document installation.\"\"\"\n+\n+    rel_path: str\n+    version: int\n+    overlay: str | None\n+    stale: bool\n+\n+\n+def find_installed_skill() -> SkillInstall | None:\n+    \"\"\"Find installed skill document metadata, or None.\"\"\"\n+    project_root = get_project_root()\n+    for rel_path in SKILL_SEARCH_PATHS:\n+        full = project_root / rel_path\n+        if not full.is_file():\n+            continue\n+        try:\n+            content = full.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\", errors=\"replace\")\n+        except OSError:\n+            continue\n+        version_match = SKILL_VERSION_RE.search(content)\n+        if not version_match:\n+            continue\n+        installed_version = int(version_match.group(1))\n+        overlay_match = SKILL_OVERLAY_RE.search(content)\n+        overlay = overlay_match.group(1) if overlay_match else None\n+        return SkillInstall(\n+            rel_path=rel_path,\n+            version=installed_version,\n+            overlay=overlay,\n+            stale=installed_version < SKILL_VERSION,\n+        )\n+    return None\n+\n+\n+def check_skill_version() -> str | None:\n+    \"\"\"Return a warning if installed skill doc is outdated.\"\"\"\n+    install = find_installed_skill()\n+    if not install or not install.stale:\n+        return None\n+    return (\n+        f\"Your desloppify skill document is outdated \"\n+        f\"(v{install.version}, current v{SKILL_VERSION}). \"\n+        \"Run: desloppify update-skill\"\n+    )\n+\n+\n+__all__ = [\n+    \"SKILL_VERSION\",\n+    \"SKILL_VERSION_RE\",\n+    \"SKILL_OVERLAY_RE\",\n+    \"SKILL_BEGIN\",\n+    \"SKILL_END\",\n+    \"SKILL_SEARCH_PATHS\",\n+    \"SKILL_TARGETS\",\n+    \"SkillInstall\",\n+    \"find_installed_skill\",\n+    \"check_skill_version\",\n+]\n*** End Patch\n"

traversal.\n+DEFAULT_EXCLUSIONS = frozenset(\n+    {\n+        \"node_modules\",\n+        \".git\",\n+        \"__pycache__\",\n+        \".venv*\",\n+        \"venv\",\n+        \".env\",\n+ exclusion patterns (called once from CLI at startup).\"\"\"\n+    runtime = current_runtime_context()\n+    runtime.exclusions = tuple(patterns)\n+    runtime.source_file_cache.clear()\n+\n+\n+def get_exclusions() -> tuple[str, ...]:\n+    \"\"\"Return current extra exclusion patterns.\"\"\"\n+    return current_runtime_context().exclusions\n+\n+\n+def enable_file_cache() -> None:\n+    \"\"\"Enable scan-scoped file content cache.\"\"\"\n+    runtime = current_runtime_context()\n+    runtime.file_text_cache.enable()\n+    runtime.cache_enabled = True\n+\n+\n+def disable_file_cache() -> None:\n+    \"\"\"Disable file content cache and free memory.\"\"\"\n+    runtime = current_runtime_context()\n+    runtime.file_text_cache.disable()\n+    runtime.cache_enabled = False\n+\n+\n+def is_file_cache_enabled() -> bool:\n+    \"\"\"Return whether scan-scoped file cache is currently enabled.\"\"\"\n+    return current_runtime_context().cache_enabled\n+\n+\n+def read_file_text(filepath: str) -> str | None:\n+    \"\"\"Read a file as text, with optional caching.\"\"\"\n+    return current_runtime_context().file_text_cache.read(filepath)\n+\n+\n+def _is_excluded_dir(name: str, rel_path: str, extra: tuple[str, ...]) -> bool:\n+    in_default_exclusions = name in DEFAULT_EXCLUSIONS or name.endswith(\".egg-info\")\n+    is_virtualenv_dir = name.startswith(\".venv\") or name.startswith(\"venv\")\n+    matches_extra_exclusion = bool(\n+        extra\n+        and any(\n+            matches_exclusion(rel_path, exclusion) or exclusion == name\n+            for exclusion in extra\n+        )\n+    )\n+    return in_default_exclusions or is_virtualenv_dir or matches_extra_exclusion\n+\n+\n+def _find_source_files_cached(\n+    path: str,\n+    extensions: tuple[str, ...],\n+    exclusions: tuple[str, ...] | None = None,\n+    extra_exclusions: tuple[str, ...] = (),\n+) -> tuple[str, ...]:\n+    \"\"\"Cached file discovery using os.walk with traversal-time pruning.\"\"\"\n+    cache_key = (path, extensions, exclusions, extra_exclusions)\n+    cache = current_runtime_context().source_file_cache\n+    cached = cache.get(cache_key)\n+    if cached is not None:\n+        return cached\n+\n+    project_root = get_project_root()\n+    root = Path(path)\n+    if not root.is_absolute():\n+        root = project_root / root\n+    all_exclusions = (exclusions or ()) + extra_exclusions\n+    ext_set = set(extensions)\n+    files: list[str] = []\n+    for dirpath, dirnames, filenames in os.walk(root):\n+        rel_dir = _normalize_path_separators(_safe_relpath(dirpath, project_root))\n+        dirnames[:] = sorted(\n+            d\n+            for d in dirnames\n+            if not _is_excluded_dir(d, rel_dir + \"/\" + d, all_exclusions)\n+        )\n+        for fname in filenames:\n+            if any(fname.endswith(ext) for ext in ext_set):\n+                full = os.path.join(dirpath, fname)\n+                rel_file = _normalize_path_separators(_safe_relpath(full, project_root))\n+                if all_exclusions and any(\n+                    matches_exclusion(rel_file, ex) for ex in all_exclusions\n+                ):\n+                    continue\n+                files.append(rel_file)\n+    result = tuple(sorted(files))\n+    cache.put(cache_key, result)\n+    return result\n+\n+\n+def find_source_files(\n+    path: str | Path, extensions: list[str], exclusions: list[str] | None = None\n+) -> list[str]:\n+    \"\"\"Find all files with given extensions under a path, excluding patterns.\"\"\"\n+    return list(\n+        _find_source_files_cached(\n+            str(path),\n+            tuple(extensions),\n+            tuple(exclusions) if exclusions else None,\n+            get_exclusions(),\n+        )\n+    )\n+\n+\n+def find_ts_files(path: str | Path) -> list[str]:\n+    return find_source_files(path, [\".ts\", \".tsx\"])\n+\n+\n+def find_tsx_files(path: str | Path) -> list[str]:\n+    return find_source_files(path, [\".tsx\"])\n+\n+\n+def find_py_files(path: str | Path) -> list[str]:\n+    return find_source_files(path, [\".py\"])\n+\n+\n+__all__ = [\n+    \"DEFAULT_EXCLUSIONS\",\n+    \"set_exclusions\",\n+    \"get_exclusions\",\n+    \"enable_file_cache\",\n+    \"disable_file_cache\",\n+    \"is_file_cache_enabled\",\n+    \"read_file_text\",\n+    \"find_source_files\",\n+    \"find_ts_files\",\n+    \"find_tsx_files\",\n+    \"find_py_files\",\n+]\n*** End Patch\n"
